F11mv_cash_sessionm SELECT s.id AS session_id,    s.store_id,    s.cashier_id,    s.opening_cash,    COALESCE(sum(t.total_amount) FILTER (WHERE t.payment_method = 'cash'::payment_method_enum), 0::numeric) AS cash_sales,    COALESCE(sum(t.total_amount) FILTER (WHERE t.payment_method = 'card'::payment_method_enum), 0::numeric) AS card_sales,    COALESCE(sum(m.amount) FILTER (WHERE m.movement_type = 'in'::cash_movement_type_enum AND m.method = 'cash'::payment_method_enum), 0::numeric) AS cash_in,    COALESCE(sum(m.amount) FILTER (WHERE m.movement_type = 'out'::cash_movement_type_enum AND m.method = 'cash'::payment_method_enum), 0::numeric) AS cash_out   FROM cash_register_sessions s     LEFT JOIN transactions t ON t.seller_id = s.cashier_id AND t.created_at >= s.opened_at     LEFT JOIN cash_movements m ON m.session_id = s.id  GROUP BY s.id, s.store_id, s.cashier_id, s.opening_cash;
F11mv_margin_productm SELECT ti.product_id,    p.name AS product_name,    sum((ti.price_at_sale - ti.cost_at_sale) * ti.quantity::numeric) AS total_margin,    sum(ti.quantity) AS total_units   FROM transaction_items ti     JOIN products p ON p.id = ti.product_id  GROUP BY ti.product_id, p.name;
F11mv_sales_dailym SELECT store_id,    date_trunc('day'::text, created_at) AS sale_date,    count(*) AS total_transactions,    sum(total_amount) AS total_sales,    sum(subtotal) AS total_subtotal   FROM transactions  GROUP BY store_id, (date_trunc('day'::text, created_at));
F11v_document_state_summaryv{security_invoker=true} SELECT 'transactions'::text AS document_type,    transactions.store_id,    transactions.status::text AS status,    count(*)::integer AS count   FROM transactions  GROUP BY transactions.store_id, transactions.statusUNION ALL SELECT 'receipts'::text AS document_type,    receipts.store_id,    receipts.status,    count(*)::integer AS count   FROM receipts  GROUP BY receipts.store_id, receipts.statusUNION ALL SELECT 'transfers'::text AS document_type,    transfers.origin_store_id AS store_id,    transfers.status::text AS status,    count(*)::integer AS count   FROM transfers  GROUP BY transfers.origin_store_id, transfers.statusUNION ALL SELECT 'devolutions'::text AS document_type,    devolutions.store_id,    devolutions.status,    count(*)::integer AS count   FROM devolutions  GROUP BY devolutions.store_id, devolutions.statusUNION ALL SELECT 'inventory_adjustments'::text AS document_type,    inventory_adjustments.store_id,    inventory_adjustments.status,    count(*)::integer AS count   FROM inventory_adjustments  GROUP BY inventory_adjustments.store_id, inventory_adjustments.statusUNION ALL SELECT 'production_orders'::text AS document_type,    production_orders.store_id,    production_orders.status,    count(*)::integer AS count   FROM production_orders  GROUP BY production_orders.store_id, production_orders.status;
F11v_global_operation_datesv{security_invoker=true} SELECT 'sale'::text AS doc_type,    transactions.id AS doc_id,    transactions.store_id,    COALESCE(transactions.cancelled_at, transactions.created_at) AS operation_date   FROM transactions  WHERE transactions.status = ANY (ARRAY['completed'::transaction_status, 'voided'::transaction_status])UNION ALL SELECT 'transfer'::text AS doc_type,    transfers.id AS doc_id,    transfers.origin_store_id AS store_id,    transfers.created_at AS operation_date   FROM transfers  WHERE transfers.status = ANY (ARRAY['PENDIENTE'::transfer_status, 'CONFIRMADA'::transfer_status])UNION ALL SELECT 'inventory_adjustment'::text AS doc_type,    inventory_adjustments.id AS doc_id,    inventory_adjustments.store_id,    inventory_adjustments.created_at AS operation_date   FROM inventory_adjustments  WHERE inventory_adjustments.status IS NOT NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'purchase_order'::text AS doc_type,    purchase_orders.id AS doc_id,    purchase_orders.store_id,    COALESCE(purchase_orders.received_at, purchase_orders.created_at) AS operation_date   FROM purchase_orders  WHERE purchase_orders.status IS NOT NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'receipt'::text AS doc_type,    receipts.id AS doc_id,    receipts.store_id,    COALESCE(receipts.reception_date::timestamp with time zone, receipts.created_at) AS operation_date   FROM receipts  WHERE receipts.status IS NOT NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'cash_closure'::text AS doc_type,    cash_closures.id AS doc_id,    cash_closures.store_id,    COALESCE(cash_closures.closed_at, cash_closures.created_at) AS operation_date   FROM cash_closuresUNION ALL SELECT 'cash_movement'::text AS doc_type,    cash_movements.id AS doc_id,    cash_movements.store_id,    cash_movements.created_at AS operation_date   FROM cash_movementsUNION ALL SELECT 'cash_session'::text AS doc_type,    cash_sessions.id AS doc_id,    cash_sessions.store_id,    COALESCE(cash_sessions.opening_at, cash_sessions.created_at) AS operation_date   FROM cash_sessionsUNION ALL SELECT 'stock_movement'::text AS doc_type,    stock_movements.id AS doc_id,    stock_movements.store_id,    stock_movements.created_at AS operation_date   FROM stock_movements;
F11v_physical_count_summaryv{security_invoker=true} SELECT pc.id,    pc.store_id,    pc.count_number,    pc.status,    pc.started_at,    pc.applied_at,    pc.total_items,    pc.total_discrepancies,    pc.total_value_discrepancy,    s.name AS store_name,    count(pci.id) AS items_with_count,    count(pci.id) FILTER (WHERE pci.difference <> 0::numeric) AS items_with_diff,    sum(pci.value_discrepancy) AS total_value_diff   FROM physical_counts pc     JOIN stores s ON s.id = pc.store_id     LEFT JOIN physical_count_items pci ON pci.count_id = pc.id  GROUP BY pc.id, s.name;
F11v_profiles_unifiedv{security_invoker=true} SELECT p.id,    p.email,    p.full_name,    p.role_id,    r.name AS role_name,    p.role AS role_enum,    p.active_store_id,    p.store_id,    p.is_active,    p.logo_url,    p.created_at,    p.updated_at   FROM profiles p     LEFT JOIN roles r ON r.id = p.role_id;
F11v_purchase_orders_cxpv{security_invoker=true} SELECT po.id AS po_id,    po.po_number,    po.store_id,    po.supplier_name,    po.supplier_id,    po.status AS po_status,    po.total_amount AS po_total,    po.created_at AS po_created_at,    r.id AS receipt_id,    r.total_cost AS receipt_total,    r.payment_status,    r.paid_amount,    r.due_date,    r.payment_terms_days,    r.paid_at,        CASE            WHEN r.payment_status = 'paid'::text THEN 'settled'::text            WHEN r.payment_status = 'partial'::text THEN 'partial'::text            WHEN r.due_date < CURRENT_DATE AND r.payment_status = 'unpaid'::text THEN 'overdue'::text            ELSE 'pending'::text        END AS cxp_status,    r.total_cost - COALESCE(r.paid_amount, 0::numeric) AS balance_due   FROM purchase_orders po     LEFT JOIN receipts r ON r.po_id = po.id  WHERE (po.status = ANY (ARRAY['partial'::purchase_status_enum, 'received'::purchase_status_enum])) AND r.id IS NOT NULL;
F11v_rls_tenant_backfill_auditv{security_invoker=true} SELECT 'products'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM products  WHERE products.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'transactions'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM transactions  WHERE transactions.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'stock_movements'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM stock_movements  WHERE stock_movements.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'inventory'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM inventory  WHERE inventory.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'profiles'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM profiles  WHERE profiles.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'commission_payments'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM commission_payments  WHERE commission_payments.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'commission_rules'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM commission_rules  WHERE commission_rules.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'receipts'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM receipts  WHERE receipts.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'workers'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM workers  WHERE workers.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'sales_transactions'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM sales_transactions  WHERE sales_transactions.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'production_orders'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM production_orders  WHERE production_orders.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'audit_events'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM audit_events  WHERE audit_events.tenant_id IS NULLUNION ALL SELECT 'price_change_history'::text AS table_name,    count(*) AS null_count   FROM price_change_history  WHERE price_change_history.tenant_id IS NULL;
F11v_stock_availablev{security_invoker=true} SELECT id AS product_id,    store_id,    stock_current,    stock_current - COALESCE(( SELECT sum(r.quantity) AS sum           FROM inventory_reservations r          WHERE r.product_id = p.id AND r.store_id = p.store_id AND r.status = 'ACTIVE'::text), 0::numeric) AS stock_available,    COALESCE(( SELECT sum(r.quantity) AS sum           FROM inventory_reservations r          WHERE r.product_id = p.id AND r.store_id = p.store_id AND r.status = 'ACTIVE'::text), 0::numeric) AS stock_reserved   FROM products p;
F11v_stock_discrepanciesv{security_invoker=true} SELECT p.id AS product_id,    p.name AS product_name,    p.store_id,    s.name AS store_name,    p.stock_current AS current_stock,    COALESCE(sum(sm.quantity_change), 0::numeric) AS expected_stock,    p.stock_current - COALESCE(sum(sm.quantity_change), 0::numeric) AS discrepancy   FROM products p     LEFT JOIN stock_movements sm ON sm.product_id = p.id AND sm.store_id = p.store_id     LEFT JOIN stores s ON s.id = p.store_id  WHERE p.is_active = true AND has_store_access(p.store_id)  GROUP BY p.id, p.name, p.store_id, s.name, p.stock_current HAVING abs(p.stock_current - COALESCE(sum(sm.quantity_change), 0::numeric)) > 0.001;
